Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The microwave operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 414. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 647 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on microwave structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 414. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 647. QED.
